Helix: Let the music begin, Virtuoso! Fate is on our side!

Helix's voice rises with fervor, but then her attention is drawn to the virtuoso. Requiem has come to stand before the hollow vessel of the captured scholar, watching in silence, whispering a few quiet words.

Requiem: ...This small section was written for you... Please take it.

*The Sage kept you alive. Now it is up to you to find your own way out of your sealed heart... This performance would not be complete without you.*

And then... Requiem does not look back.

Requiem: I'm sorry, Helix. From here on, the ritual doesn't need you.

Helix: ...Huh?

With a soft push from Requiem, a chill sweeps in without warning. Helix's feet are sealed to the ground by frost, and the one standing before her slips beyond her reach.

Requiem: She needs a soul far stronger than yours. You're too fragile to hold the resonance.

Requiem: Stay here. I'll handle the rest.

Helix: ...No... No!

Helix: Did I mess up? Am I not strong enough? Not faithful enough? Is it because of ${PlayerName}?

Helix: I'm sorry, I'm so sorry, please! Tell me what I did wrong and I'll fix it! Don't take this away from me, please, Virtuoso!

The girl cries until tears cover her face. She is such a wretched sight that even Requiem cannot harden herself against it, and at last she goes to her and folds her into a gentle embrace.

Requiem: ...I'm sorry, Helix. That was a lie. You didn't do anything wrong. I was the one who wasn't being fair.

Requiem: I'm sorry. I heard what you wanted so badly, and I still got in the way.

Requiem: Don't come after me. And don't call me Virtuoso anymore. If we ever meet again...

Requiem: Call me Requiem.

Helix: Huh...?

The enormous Apostle stoops in docility before Requiem. She steps up onto its broad back. Before setting out, she glances back toward Helix one final time.

That face holds bewilderment and fear. Helix had long since determined to offer every grief and terror she carried to the Great Presence, and yet Requiem can still make it out beneath the zealous, seamless chanting. A single faint note of discord the chorus has yet to smother.

You will find it back... the voice that is your own.

Requiem: Go. To the Firmament Black Ring.

Bit by bit, the Aurum Secretum shell, steeped in black and red, deforms and extends outward. At the virtuoso's bidding, its long wings stir an invisible wind and bear it up into the heights, unfolding like petals, trembling like the strings of an instrument.

Helix: No—!

The field of battle has been brought under control, though the remaining few points of conflict abruptly intensify. You understand what it portends: the final moment is at hand. Their target is BR-003.

The first Aurum Secretum Apostle dragon has already breached the encirclement, bearing the virtuoso upon it, and with the Cursed Melody's blessing it drives straight toward BR-003.

FAC: ...?! The Hypercubes are vibrating abnormally. Anti-Mania output is plummeting... 86%, 62%, 34%... What could cause this?! We've never seen anything like it. What in the world... is that abnormal resonance...

Within the blue crystal's core, a familiar light begins to seep through. It's red and ominous, as if a comrade you had leaned on all this while has finally revealed, on this very day, whom they truly serve.

FAC: It's Mania?!

At the same time, the outer barrier of the BR-003 Observation Hub is at last forced open by the Aurum Secretum Apostle dragon. In its wake, the surviving sorcerers and Puppets rush through an enormous fissure all at once, spilling into the Hub.

The Hub has reinforced its defenses, but the fissure cannot be shut at once. Confronted by this tide of Undergrounders who have cast everything aside for a single, desperate end, Lemma finds herself in peril once more.

Lemma: The Apostles of Ashes are smashing through the outer line! The Cursed Melody resonance is eating through the Black Ring's inner wall. Shield is overloading... E26 is about to fall!!

Lemma: Damn it, they used the fissures to drop the monsters here... I didn't see that coming...

FAC: EDGE-07! Fall back, now!

Lemma: Go without me! I'm staying. I'm almost there on the spacetime curve... just a little more... There's still a chance... I'm not letting those Underground scumbags—

Boom! At the thunderous blast, Lemma cries out by instinct, and yet in the very next moment the vast fissure contorts, then slowly draws closed. Countless Undergrounders caught mid-assault vanish then and there, swallowed by the spacetime turbulence.

Something about this feels familiar, but there is no time to dwell on it. Lemma has her mission still. If she can finish the spacetime curve, she can reverse the whole battle in one stroke, sending these monsters straight back where they came from.

If only she had a little more time—

??: Let her in. I'll take it from here.

Lemma: You are...?!

??: They'll understand soon enough... not all Black Rings answer to Mania.

At the other end, the Sage is the first to sense Hush's presence.

Evelleon: ...She's here.

Evelleon: Good. I'm curious to see what you can do with its power.

## N12-13 Tearful Sunset

Flawed Schorl: ...Do you have a plan after entering the Black Ring? Please brief me first, including contingencies.

Chief: You sound worried.

Flawed Schorl: I need you to confirm something. Do you believe that once you're inside the Black Ring, within range of the virtuoso's performance, you can use the Cursed Melody to trigger a rewind—even if the mission fails?

Flawed Schorl: I'd urge caution. This performance is nothing like the ones before. The Black Ring resonates with the entire city; it will amplify her attunement power without limit. We don't know where the rewind anchor is. We don't know if you can withstand it. You've already come close to breaking down once.

You smile. The expression startles Schorl, as though it has not seen you smile in a very long while.

**【Advance】**

- I'm not that addicted. I quit.

**end**

Your smile fades, and the events of not long ago tug at you once more. That weariness is still there, close enough to touch.

Chief: Rewind is convenient, I won't lie. I've used it more times than I can count. There was a point when I depended on it.

Chief: But looking back, it only works when things are going well. The moment I hit something hard, hit an obstacle I just can't get past... it turns into a nightmare. Like a curse.

Chief: Every failure, magnified again and again, was hammered into my brain like a nail. Restart. Run. Restart. Run. I was too scared to listen, too scared to look. My world got smaller and smaller. I was stuck in the past, sinking deeper every time.

Chief: Time looping back on itself, over and over, until somehow I was the only one left walking that road. At my lowest, I actually wondered...

Chief: ...maybe those Undergrounders lost their minds the same way I almost did...

Flawed Schorl: ...Assessment complete. Rest assured, you are nothing like them right now.

Chief: Yeah. Weirdly enough, without it, I feel freer.

9th: So, lead the way, Chief. Take us to victory.

Security Personnel: Go on ahead. We've got your back. Just keep moving forward and take them down, as many times as it takes!

Nightingale: Chief, all preparations are complete. Standing by for your orders. We move when you say the word.

Chief: No more wondering how to do it over next time. When you've only got one chance to live, you make peace with failure quickly. And only then, beyond the horror of it, can you start to—

Chief: See something new.

You turn to look at Schorl.

Chief: That Shepherd, BEA. She's coming. That's inevitable, isn't it?

Flawed Schorl: Yes.

Chief: But her coming doesn't mean the end, does it?

Chief: I once killed her with my own hands.

Flawed Schorl: ......

Flawed Schorl: ...And how much did that cost you?

You shrug.

**【Advance】**

- Enough that I got banned from ever doing it again.

**end**

Chief: But it proves one thing—she can be defeated.

Schorl grows almost perfectly still and angles itself toward you.

Chief: In the rewinds I've been through, it's my consciousness going back in time into my own body. So there's barely any friction. But that doesn't mean jumping into someone else's body is free. The longer the time gap, the more it hurts, even when it's just me. Now picture two totally different people.

Chief: Whatever vessel the Underground picks, it still has to hold a Mania entity strong enough to swallow a Black Ring. The attunement between the two must be agonizing. Which means the second she descends, she's at her weakest.

Chief: Locate every offering inside the Ring, and we clear them out one by one. If she's too valuable, we capture her instead. Put the Rules on her, just like what you did to me. What do you think? I guarantee they'll work on her too.

Chief: Let her come. If we get there before the believers do, who knows? Maybe even I can kill a newborn "god."

Flawed Schorl: ...

Flawed Schorl: A very imaginative plan. I see the past isn't weighing on you anymore. You've found the courage to look forward. But what if you fail?

Once more, a smile forms on your lips. Strange, how effortless it turns out to be.

Chief: Let's leave that to the future me and everyone else left standing. We'll find a way together. This world has heroes to spare. Who says it can't go on without a do-over?

Chief: Let's go. We can't let the virtuoso beat us there. I still have questions for her.

You ready your defenses and weapons.

The question lingers with you. Why did Requiem go out of her way to destroy every blood crystal before she left, cutting off any chance of a rewind?

Is it because you stand on different sides? In every future, she remains loyal to the Underground. The moment she chose to play and summon the Shepherd, she set your two sides on an inescapable collision course. Yet when you truly die, you always find yourself brought back. No one else would do it.

If she really sees you as an enemy, she will never let you go across all those futures.

But if she doesn't...

You do not want to keep making these one-sided guesses.

Chief: She always said she'd listen to any voice, for the sake of the music. Well, now it's her turn to speak.

Chief: She's been silent long enough. Through her music or words, it doesn't matter; she has to speak. This time, I'll be the one listening.

Flawed Schorl: I see.

Flawed Schorl: Lifting all top-level administrative restrictions. HUSH units, form up immediately. Mobilize all Hypercube reserves. This is the moment of reckoning, Chief.

You nod.

**【Advance】**

- Let's go. Time to deliver the Underground Shepherd into the world.

**end**

You gaze ahead, and with the assembled force, you charge through the storm and dive into the Black Ring—

The Apostle, armed in Aurum Secretum, dives headlong into the Black Ring. The light around it is swallowed in an instant. Mania beats against the Aurum Secretum shell as though it were solid, while wild currents of air and shards of black crystal streak past the edge of vision, only to be held back by the barrier.

At last, it strikes the ground with a thunderous crash and gently lowers the virtuoso from its back. Then, with a piercing cry, it hurtles toward its true objective.

Alone at the center of the observatory. Tracking the direction the Apostle departed, Requiem raises her eyes skyward.

Within the Black Ring, the colossal structure has been torn from its foundations and hangs inverted in the sky, as though it might come crashing down at any moment. Shrouded in ominous corruption and red light, it trembles and resonates with the Cursed Melody.

Requiem: ...So this is the Black Ring.

Apostle of Ashes: ROARRR—

It clambers onto the inverted structure and drives deep within, rending with its fangs, slicing with its wings. Within the crumbling domed ruin, blue light throbs without pause. Blue and red collide, and the buildings still clinging to the upturned ground are shattered to pieces.

No one has yet noticed the frail virtuoso on the small observatory. Showered in the crimson glow, Requiem slowly closes her eyes. In the darkness, countless voices pour through the Mania and flood into her mind.

Requiem: I can hear them...

Requiem: I can hear...all of them now...

...It's a strange world indeed, as chaotic and clamorous as the one outside. And yet here, she can hear with far greater clarity. It is as though this is the only place where all stand equal, where anyone may raise their voice and make themselves heard.

She is no different. Carried in upon the music, the voices that have stirred so long within Requiem's heart are at last set free, one by one. In the flood of time beneath the Ring's light, each rediscovers its own era, then journeys to the fissure to call for the one to whom it once made a promise.

Wandering Virtuoso: Well, it's just a broken leg. No big deal, no big deal. I'm just out a month's pay. Call it a vacation.

Wandering Virtuoso: Look, my hands are fine! I can still busk on the street! If anything, a limp'll probably get me more tips. Now, what should I play?

Wandering Virtuoso: Something loud and rowdy! How about that? Festival's coming up, let's have some fun! And you, cheer up too!

Soldier Virtuoso: Of course I'm going. The bravest people in all of DisCity are on that front line, and that makes it the most exciting, most vibrant place to be. Miss it and you've missed the whole era!

Soldier Virtuoso: Don't worry. Even if I fall, a thousand more will take up the horn and keep blowing.

Soldier Virtuoso: You and this city-state will hear that victory call. I'm sure of it... I'm sure!

Exiled Virtuoso: Every note will carry the weight of their sin... Everyone who doesn't come back will live on in my music. And if one day I'm not here either...

Exiled Virtuoso: You will be.

Exiled Virtuoso: You hold on to things even harder than I do.

Punk Virtuoso: That blast was insane! This whole ******* DisCity is coming apart today! All those lapdogs are finished. Now it's your turn. I'll keep the drums, you do your job. That was the deal.

Punk Virtuoso: Where the hell are you?!

Listen. Listen with care. Only here do hearts lie open, with no barrier between them. They say a virtuoso's life is too brief, that they see and hear too little, that they cannot distinguish right from wrong or discern the course of fate.

But look around this place. Who is not so? People are grains of sand, each longing to catch the light and gleam, and each so easily lost to the mud and the tide. Swept into the vortex, sunk into the swamp, they become carrion and festering sores that swell and roar.

The virtuoso keeps listening. When she listens closely... she catches a faint thread of sound.

*We will devote our entire lives to seeking the best answer for the majority of humanity.*

*We are willing to give all, even if only one among us reaches the end.*

*She hears it clearly now.*

Requiem: *You who have buried yourselves underground, exiled from the world, desperate to stay lucid, willing to throw away everything for the promises you kept, even if it meant sinking into the abyss... I can finally hear your grief, and the wishes you've never let go of. But...*

Before her mind's eye appears the look on Helix's face, stunned and utterly lost.

Requiem: *A vessel forged from lies could never hold her. Let Helix go. I'll bring Bea back myself. Your Shepherd will return.*

Requiem: *I won't break the hope you've held on to through all these years of darkness. The virtuosos have been waiting so long for it. It's a promise made, and it's the small light that you once saved me with.*

Requiem: *And now...*

The shackles are close now. With herself laid bare to the Black Ring in this way, she can feel it.

That person is on the way, drawing closer. If fate truly exists, it must have a fine sense of humor.

It's as if something she has yearned for, a treasure waited for so long, is delivered into her hands in a way she never could have foreseen.

Requiem: *I've found my own listener too, sooner than you told me. Surprising, isn't it?*

<ins>Requiem: *To play for him is also my wish. Just a small one... I don't think you'll mind.*</ins><br><ins>Requiem: *To play for her is also my wish. Just a small one... I don't think you'll mind.*</ins>

<ins>The violin lies in Requiem's hands, and the strange little thing he hid inside it still sounds faintly on. Requiem is fond of it. She lifts the violin, raises her hand, and closes her eyes—</ins><br><ins>The violin lies in Requiem's hands, and the strange little thing she hid inside it still sounds faintly on. Requiem is fond of it. She lifts the violin, raises her hand, and closes her eyes—</ins>

In the chaotic white noise of the age, she hears the symphony she has been seeking.

Requiem: Come. The music is written. I've listened to others for so many years, too many to count. But I've finally found it. Reaching across time to land here, this is the melody this era has given me, and this is my own movement at last.

Requiem: Now... I'd like you all to listen to me.

FAC: S-125's playing is resonating with every infected person in the city! M-values are skyrocketing... It's BR-003! The Black Ring is magnifying the resonance! Spacetime is bending... no, time itself is bending! Something's coming!!!

Nightingale: The Hypercubes in the Black Ring are still failing. If we pool all of what we have left, it's just enough for one last assault. There's only one minute left. We have to stop the music and shut her down from inside the Black Ring!

The music swells. Savage contamination shrieks all around. You tighten your grip on the handhold and look down upon the city beyond the Ring, twisting and caving in on itself, and upon that one small figure beneath the distorted halo of light.

Every Hypercube weapon carried aboard the helicopter stands fully primed, waiting only for your command...

Flawed Schorl: The main force has moved to neutralize the Apostle's threat to the Black Ring. HUSH is tracking down the hidden offerings. Give the order, Chief. You said you would.

Flawed Schorl: If you won't rewind, then seize every chance and fight for victory.

Chief: ......

Chief: Of course.

You reach out and act on what you hold to be right.

**【Advance】**

- Focus fire.

**end**

FAC: Massive Mania intrusion, repeat, massive Mania intrusion! Spacetime coordinates are gone. Can't pinpoint the source. We're picking up shackles activity inside the Ring. The signature... it's off the charts!!!

FAC: Chief! Chief?!!

The space within the Black Ring has always churned with storms, but now it rages with a madness unlike anything before. Stirred by the Cursed Melody, everything inside the field has become one vast vortex.

Flawed Schorl: —*ksshh*— HUSH forces are still clearing the offerings. No clear successor has emerged yet. —*ksshh*— Chief—

**【Advance】**

- Keep at it... I'll deal with the virtuoso.

**end**

What were you just fighting?

The enormous, dreadful form has vanished. Was it repelled? You glance about. Upon the triangular platform, no evidence remains that such a colossal thing ever stood there.

But your mind, your nerves, every last cell cries out that she is close. That she is here.

Dazed Voice: Chief...

The shackles blaze around you, red enough to sting the eyes, as though every Sinner bound to you has gathered at your side to hold you up and shield you.

At last, you find your balance, and you see it plainly: not far from where you stand, an enemy stands alone to face you.

Requiem draws the bow, quicker and quicker still. Strings and bow alike shine crimson as fresh blood. The frenzy of decades, of whole generations, spills forth in a ceaseless eruption.

**【Advance】**

- *Stop.*

**end**

The musician has laid herself entirely open. All the consciousness the Black Ring has dissolved becomes her nourishment, and the boundary of her humanity disintegrates at a terrifying pace. The dodder vines strive to pour life into her—

And yet they appear instead as though death is winding itself ever more tightly about her, and that body of hers is withering and fading as you watch.

**【Advance】**

- *The attunement is over. What else do you want?*

**end**

Your body moves of its own accord, hauling itself toward her with leaden strides.

She must be stopped. This resolve gathers all the consciousness within you into one.

Requiem: ...!!

From a dozen meters away, the crimson light of the shackles cuts straight through the violin. The bow snaps. Red strings scatter. Perhaps it is because you once repaired this instrument yourself. The shackles know every weakness it has, striking with perfect precision, tearing it apart in an instant. That nightmare of a melody finally breaks off.

And at the moment the violin is destroyed, you hear a voice drifting through the particles in the air around you.

Voice from Above: Well done.

At the crown of the Black Ring, a flash of blue light crosses the inverted observatory, and then it shudders with a heavy tremor. The Apostle of Ashes that had settled there utters a keening cry, wrenched from its perch by something unseen, torn apart, and reduced to dust in the Black Ring's suddenly raging storm.

The whole Black Ring is freed from the music's grip, as if its rightful master has come home to reclaim the throne. And the first thing it does is...

The modest triangular observatory beneath you collapses inward without warning, breaking into several pieces. A single floating fragment bears you up securely, while the virtuoso, now stripped of her violin...

FAC: Shackles have pierced S-125's defenses. The Cursed Melody has been halted. A direct hit confirmed from the observatory. S-125 is falling!

${PlayerName}: Requiem?!

**【Choose One】**

- Save her

  Your body leaps without the slightest hesitation, plunging into the void.

  The gale tears at your ears, and you pay it no mind at all. You weave past each stone that surges up to receive you, stretching with all your strength toward the one who falls.

  ${PlayerName}: Give me your hand!! Now—

- Do nothing (Fake option)

  At the moment your decision is made, an unaccountable fury bursts forth. Crimson light splits from your hand and shatters the boulder you clung to. What follows is agony tearing through your entire body, as blue light pierces your skin and congeals into a cage.

  And yet this body does not hesitate in the least. It follows her into the void.

  The gale tears at the ears, and it is paid no mind at all. The body weaves past each stone that surges up to receive it, stretching with reckless desperation toward the falling virtuoso.

  ${PlayerName} & ??: Give me your hand!! Now—

**end**

Closer. Just a little closer.

There are still words left unspoken. Whatever choice she makes this time, it must be made clear. You will listen.

A little further, and she reaches back toward you. You will catch her—

It is only the briefest touch, followed by a gentle push away, as though she were merely measuring the distance between you.

At last, it's close enough to speak, close enough to hear each other's hearts without the Cursed Melody.

Requiem: I've found my own movement.

Requiem: *Since I stepped out of that small world, I've heard so many interesting sounds.*

Requiem: *Bright, fierce, unshakable... sounds that refuse to bend, refuse to let go. The sharper they are, the more they cut against each other.*

Requiem: *And still, how beautiful they are. Every sound that has ever touched my heart lingers, slowly weaving into something of my own.*

Requiem: *I know that not everything can be resolved. Reality is no symphony. Chaos is the truest sound of all.*

Requiem: *But for now, at least, this is the movement I have written. And here...*

Requiem: *Every promise will be kept, and the sounds I love will find their harmony.*

${PlayerName} & ??: What are you trying to do... Stop!

Requiem: *Almost there. The violin you gave me is already broken, but it's okay...*

Requiem: *I'm still here.*

${PlayerName} & ??: No—!!

Requiem: My movement is finished...

Requiem: I'm so glad... you are here to hear it.

The virtuoso herself is the instrument. And with that, the closing passage is complete. The final note drifts toward the end, merging into the Black Ring, the Mania, and the surging currents of time and space.

Hecate: Chief!

Lemma: The emergency fissure is open! This way!

The falling hero is caught by their companions. The lone warrior returns to safety.

And so time returns to its rightful course. The long night is past, and dawn arrives once again.

August 12, N.F. 116

06:23 AM

The Cursed Melody's eruption, which had lasted for hours, ceases all at once. The Underground's scheme to invade BR-003 and use its resonance to spread the melody across the entire city has been thoroughly thwarted. The MBCC Chief, who commanded from the front lines in person, has returned safely after the battle.

Max King: This is Speaker Max King. To every citizen, thank you for your trust and your dedication. And to every department, thank you for the fight, the sacrifice, and the strength you gave when we needed it most. I am officially declaring: the source of the Cursed Melody contamination has been destroyed, and the city's state of emergency is over.

Max King: ...I know these days have taken their toll on each of you. But we stood our ground, and we're still standing.

Max King: We've proven that disaster no longer leaves us powerless. Time after time, we've risen together. This is victory born of courage. Let us believe in it: the darkness is fading, and the dawn is ours to claim.

With the performance brought to an end, every node of the Mania network was purged. The Aurum Secretum Apostle that had breached the Black Ring was destroyed, and those that had not yet entered were swiftly eradicated by General Catherine Augustus Andohar upon her timely return.

In the aftermath, a great many members of the Sillage Society are exposed and subjected to investigation. Quinn is shut down for restructuring. Its president, Eirene Campbell, a Fraser spy and supporter of the Underground, remains unaccounted for.

Dozens of captured Underground sorcerers and high-ranking operatives are transferred by EDGE to a classified containment site. There, they will become prime research subjects for the foremost experts in the field of consciousness.

This can be confirmed as the gravest blow the Underground has suffered since Eclipse Operation.

Adelaide is moved to a cell of stricter confinement. The Sage's mark on him has faded considerably. The toys he lent his sister must all be broken by now. The Mania flower on his hand still answers faintly. He accepts it all with quiet composure, having long since foreseen this ending.

What saddens him, and yet in some way consoles him, is what comes to him through the bond of shared blood.

Adelaide: Rest now, Eirene. This side of the world is not yours to bear... Sleep well. Sleep until the one who truly belongs beside you returns.

Adelaide: Now then...destiny awaits...

The virtuoso is lost within the Black Ring. And now, when the Cursed Melody she once performed is played again, it stirs no Mania at all.

Word arrives from the Third Observatorium: the Underground virtuoso, Sinner S-125, has been consumed entirely by the ritual and confirmed dead.

Her remains and residual Mania are absorbed completely by BR-003. Drawing on the theory of cross-temporal attunement, a research initiative into sealing consciousness entities is launched at once, likewise placed under the charge of EDGE.

Should it succeed, the so-called lineage of the Underground virtuosos will be severed forever. Without a medium for attunement, the summoning ritual will never again call back the consciousness they so desperately sought.

Since the sin has been left in the past, let it be sealed away in history. Forever silent, buried beneath the ground.

—Do not obstruct humanity's advance into the future.

Helix: ......

Mr. E: Today, we step out of the shadows and stand together to honor a valiant warrior, a beloved ally, and the greatest virtuoso to ever grace this world. High above the Black Ring, she stood alone against our enemy, giving every last ounce of herself to complete the sacred calling.

Mr. E: Her life was brief, but it burned brilliantly, never for herself. She marched unyieldingly toward her destiny, offered herself wholly to the Divine, shattered time's cage, and brought our Shepherd home. Now, we are tasked with finding the herald who has returned.

Evelleon: *...I never imagined you'd go this far. You always smiled and listened, and even I forgot... you were more unyielding than any of us.*

Evelleon: *Giving the final piece to someone else, leaving only your broken violin behind... Bea will be absolutely livid when she comes back.*

Evelleon: *...But that melody was so beautiful. I wish I could hear it again...*

Mr. E: Rest now, Requiem. Sleep peacefully until the end comes. Your music will not fade, and your melody will echo through eternity as the great one comes back. When we reunite... we will not let you down.

Everyone has come back.

The handful of days since the Undergrounder's capture have felt as long as years. To pass through something so violent without suffering any parting of life and death leaves them all marveling, deeply grateful.

Eugene: Hey, we all made it back safe, right? Cheer up! Need my help with the celebration? Or should we wait for Chief? Hey, don't be mad...

Wynn: I am NOT mad! I'm happy. Really, I am! It's just... sob... I'm just so glad you're all okay...

At the Observation Hub, EDGE-07 dashes tensely between the various monitors and communication devices. At last, the health data for a certain someone arrives from MBCC, and she is able to breathe easy.

Lemma: Readings are slowly ticking back up. That was way too close... At that range, a direct Cursed Melody impact should have been fatal, given the historical data of the shackles. Good thing it worked out...

Lemma: ...But really, there are limits to how reckless you can be.

Elsewhere, lying silently inside the immense and disorderly archive of battle data she will soon be reviewing, is a small set of anomalous dimensional fissure signatures from the BR-003 battle.

Elvira: ...Hecate? Are you okay?

Hecate: ...Chief still isn't awake.

<ins>Elvira: He was too close to the Sillage Society. Might have touched something he shouldn't have.</ins><br><ins>Elvira: She was too close to the Sillage Society. Might have touched something she shouldn't have.</ins>

Hecate: And Hella, too...

Not long after ${PlayerName} entered the Black Ring, the Scourge mark began to show obvious signs of anomaly. Since every Sinner was strictly shielded by the Cursed Melody blocker, the only thing capable of crossing all barriers to produce such an effect was the shackles.

Hecate: What really happened in BR-003 when that music played...

<ins>Elvira does not fully grasp the worry. To her mind, ${PlayerName} is already back, right here beside them, close enough to touch. He will wake, of course. He always does.</ins><br><ins>Elvira does not fully grasp the worry. To her mind, ${PlayerName} is already back, right here beside them, close enough to touch. She will wake, of course. She always does.</ins>

But she gently holds Hecate's hand all the same.

Elvira: ...It'll be fine. Once Chief ${PlayerName} wakes up, everything will be alright.

Chief: I'll be back. Trust me.

At last, Hecate rises and steps outside. She makes her way down the corridor, only to run into someone unexpected at the doorway.

Hecate: You are...?

<ins>???: ...You should all head back. He needs to rest now.</ins><br><ins>???: ...You should all head back. She needs to rest now.</ins>

???: Everything will be fine.

<ins>EDGE regards ${PlayerName}'s condition with great concern. A private ward, careful treatment. At first, everyone prepares for lingering aftereffects, but all the readings soon settle. The spectra align, with no abnormal interference. Everyone believes he will wake before long.</ins><br><ins>EDGE regards ${PlayerName}'s condition with great concern. A private ward, careful treatment. At first, everyone prepares for lingering aftereffects, but all the readings soon settle. The spectra align, with no abnormal interference. Everyone believes she will wake before long.</ins>

Perhaps even if the melody of this era were to leave, it would always find its way back.

"Chief": Cough... cough...

<ins>He woke up.</ins><br><ins>She woke up.</ins>

Dear Chief, I'm going back now. I never found the right moment to say goodbye, so I'm writing this letter, hoping it finds you in time.

You've always been a wellspring of inspiration for me. The melody came together so quickly, it almost surprised me. Now I have to go. I made a promise, and I intend to keep it.

They are the ones who helped me, the ones I've always admired. I imagine... they're also the "enemies" you have to stand against, aren't they?

You're always watchful. Through turn after turn of the same wheel, you must have glimpsed horrors that words can't hold, and wandered through mazes with no exit in sight.

So rest, just for a moment. I've thought of a good idea.

It may not be a good one, just a small wish to slow her down, to keep her from racing forward.

The virtuosos told me that Bea is quite stubborn, even fiercer than you. No one can pull her back.

But then again... what if?

Would you give it a try?

The same way you laid down your burdens, time after time, just to come closer to me. Perhaps you and she can meet in the middle too.

Thank you, for coming to me again and again, for looking at me, for listening to me.

In the future I cannot see, I'm sure you have walked even longer roads.

But I believe that wherever you go, whomever you face, you'll remain exactly who you are.

And that's more than enough.

There's no need to trace boundaries. Fate has already written enough goodbyes, so let it do what it will. We just need to keep reaching out, every chance we get.

I'm not saying farewell. I might not be able to hide what shows on my face.

So instead, I look forward to the day...we meet again.

Requiem: When the music plays again... I'll come back to you.

*${PlayerName}*: ..."I'll come back to you." Tch.

*${PlayerName}*: As if anyone remembers your music. As if anyone reads the letter you left behind...

*${PlayerName}*: You fool...

<ins>The shackles shred the letter with ease. As it crumples into ash, a trace of regret stirs in him.</ins><br><ins>The shackles shred the letter with ease. As it crumples into ash, a trace of regret stirs in her.</ins>

But from the shadows, a voice cuts through every sentiment.

Voice in the Shadows: For now, I'd advise you to limit your use of the shackles.

Voice in the Shadows: That direct, close-range hit from the Cursed Melody inside the Black Ring has already triggered the Rules' defensive protocols. In the near future, even minor Mania fluctuations could provoke an overreaction and damage your body.

*${PlayerName}*: ......

There is something truly sickening about the way he speaks.

*${PlayerName}*: That's the talk of someone kept in a cage.

<ins>*${PlayerName}*: ...Where is he?</ins><br><ins>*${PlayerName}*: ...Where is she?</ins>

<ins>Voice in the Shadows: Somewhere you can never touch right now. He's safe.</ins><br><ins>Voice in the Shadows: Somewhere you can never touch right now. She's safe.</ins>

Voice in the Shadows: Thanks to your virtuoso friend.

The shackles pierce clean through the man's left shoulder, and the light carves a fracture into the wall behind him. Seven or eight meters stand between the two of them.

<ins>Once again, the irritating blue light drives into *${PlayerName}*'s hand. He pays it no heed at all, his glare fixed on the offender pinned to the wall.</ins><br><ins>Once again, the irritating blue light drives into *${PlayerName}*'s hand. She pays it no heed at all, her glare fixed on the offender pinned to the wall.</ins>

*${PlayerName}*: Do you have any idea who you're speaking to, boy?

The white cloth is instantly stained red, though the wound stays far from both the heart and any artery. So the man casts it one look and lets it be.

Voice in the Shadows: Naturally. Do look after this body. And mind your words and actions.

!!

A blue triangle kindles in his pupils. When *${PlayerName}* catches sight of it, it is already too late. It engraves itself into the eyes of the one who gazes upon it, and then—

<ins>Consciousness is momentarily drawn out of the flesh, projected into a space of utter silence. In this place, blue light lances through the crimson shackles, striking and striking, holding the man fast.</ins><br><ins>Consciousness is momentarily drawn out of the flesh, projected into a space of utter silence. In this place, blue light lances through the crimson shackles, striking and striking, holding the woman fast.</ins>

The man in white enters as well, appearing younger here, his form grown strangely insubstantial. He observes the cornered creature before him as if assessing how well the Rules hold. Once satisfied, he gives a single nod, expressionless.

Voice in the Shadows: We both still have a long journey ahead. For your own sake, make sure no third party learns of this.

<ins>Of course, *${PlayerName}* could free himself with ease. But... he knows this place.</ins><br><ins>Of course, *${PlayerName}* could free herself with ease. But... she knows this place.</ins>

*${PlayerName}*: Prism Zero...? You are... EDGE?

EDGE-01: In the flesh.

EDGE-01: We finally meet. Welcome to the forefront of time itself. What should I call you?

EDGE-01: The Shepherd of the Forsaken.

${PlayerName}?: Cough... cough, cough...

One misstep, and your body is jostled, nearly breaking into pieces. Every cell feels strange to you. Your hands and feet will not obey. You sway, on the brink of falling at any instant.

...How long has it been since then?

Where is... the music from just now?

You look around, yet the sound that drew you here leaves no trace behind.

All you hear is the vast clamor rushing toward you.

Passerby: Eastside expansion! Eastside expansion! Invest now, get in while you can!

Beggar: I got laid off after I got hurt on the job. Got kids at home... Please, sir, spare some change... please...

Passerby: Expert instruction from the metropoles, your ticket to study abroad and a glorious return! Step right up!

People come and go, and every kind of voice pours into your ears. The world resembles a colossal picture. A grand celebration sweeps toward you, engulfs you, then rushes past with a roar, leaving you at the roadside as a mere onlooker.

${PlayerName}?: ...Who? What are they saying?

${PlayerName}?: What's going on?

You lift your head in a daze. The sky is familiar, and yet not. Then—

${PlayerName}?: !

The music grazes your ear a final time and drifts away into nothing. Then a voice reaches you, one you do not recognize, rising from a skull that is not your own.

${PlayerName}?: ...Where... am I?

Loudspeaker: Let it rip! It's the golden age after the Nightfall! Happy birthday, DisCity!

Loudspeaker: Happy 25th, you glorious bastard!

N.F. 50

Land of Dawn

DisCity

## The Dying Echo - EX

### SEQ-T-1 Glimmer of Nightfall

> When the long night first falls, people will salvage the first glimmer from the ruins, holding it high as a substitute for the stars.

### SEQ-T-2 The Golden Age

> Dawn rises as promised, yet it only reveals chains of prosperity and a wasteland with no owner.

### SEQ-T-3 Blood and Fire

> Blood taught people to guard that which they love, while fire burned away any option to retreat.

### SEQ-T-4 Iron Oath

> By the time a new throne is forged from steel, the horns of victory have yet to fade.

### SEQ-T-5 Time of Nothingness

> Perhaps what humanity truly seeks is not an empty present, but everything that came before.

## The Dying Echo - Boss

### DEFECT-POD Revelation

> Revelation comes from beyond fate, born of the turmoil in human hearts. Is it a horn heralding the end, or the echo of countless ages...?
> Only one thing is certain: as the four tunes turn in an endless cycle, beneath the darkness of the dome, those brilliant lights have remained suppressed—until today.
